# Event 19 consolidated registry effects.
# This is the sole dedicated Event 19 registry file. It owns Evolution III's
# ordinary component registry plus the initial Evolution IV integrations. The
# generic Chaos-family contract remains event-agnostic: a future family defines
# its complete provider registration and Event 19 callbacks in that family's
# own integration surface, then calls its registration from its existing parent
# startup path. No future family requires an edit to this file, another Event 19
# registry file, or an Event 19 family-list row.

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_dispatch_static_token = {
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_token_provider_[PROVIDER_ID] = yes
		}
		PROVIDER_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_provider_id|.0]"
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_current_row = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_id value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_row_ids^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_token_id value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_token_ids^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_provider_id value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_provider_ids^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_gate_provider_id value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_gate_provider_ids^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_slot_kind value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_slot_kinds^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_groups^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_equipment_profile value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_equipment_profiles^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_quality_weight value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_weights^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_compatibility_classes^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility_classes^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burdens^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_risks^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_primary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_same_support_type_primary^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_secondary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_same_support_type_secondary^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_tertiary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_same_support_type_tertiary^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_dispatch_static_token = yes
}

	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_ids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_counts
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_token_ids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_type_ids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_row_indices
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_token_ids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_static_tokens
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_provider_ids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_slot_kinds

	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_equipment_profiles
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_quality_weights

	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_mobility_classes

	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }

}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_quality_roll_bias = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control < constant:infantry_spawn_control.low } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_poor_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.low_control_poor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control < constant:infantry_spawn_control.strong } } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_advanced_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.high_control_advanced }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_exceptional_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.high_control_exceptional }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.numbers compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_poor_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.numbers_poor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.discipline compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_standard_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.discipline_standard }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_advanced_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.discipline_advanced }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.anything compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_poor_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.anything_poor }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_exceptional_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.anything_exceptional }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_coherence_roll_bias = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control < constant:infantry_spawn_control.low } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_chaotic_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.low_control_chaotic }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control < constant:infantry_spawn_control.strong } } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_coherent_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.high_control_coherent }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion < constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.severe } } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_chaotic_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.severe_congestion_chaotic }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.numbers compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_chaotic_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.numbers_chaotic }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.discipline compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_coherent_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.discipline_coherent }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_specialist_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.discipline_specialist }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.anything compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_chaotic_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.anything_chaotic }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_specialist_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.anything_specialist }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_context value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_context.claimant_request compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_chaotic_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.claimant_chaotic }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_combat_count_roll_bias = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion < constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.severe } } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_bloated_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.severe_congestion_bloated }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_count_generation > constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_repeated_request_tail_weight value = infantry_spawn_request_count_generation }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_repeated_request_tail_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.repeated_request_tail_per_request }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_tiny_weight value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_repeated_request_tail_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_bloated_weight value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_repeated_request_tail_weight }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.numbers compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_heavy_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.numbers_heavy }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_bloated_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.numbers_bloated }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.discipline compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_light_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.discipline_light }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_full_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.discipline_full }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.firepower compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_heavy_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.firepower_heavy }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.anything compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_tiny_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.anything_tiny }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_bloated_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.anything_bloated }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_context value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_context.claimant_request compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_bloated_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.claimant_bloated }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_support_count_roll_bias = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.firepower compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_three_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.firepower_support_three }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_four_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.firepower_support_four }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_five_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.firepower_support_five }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.anything compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_zero_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.anything_support_zero }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_five_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_bias.anything_support_five }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_independent_axes = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_poor_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.quality_poor }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_standard_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.quality_standard }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_advanced_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.quality_advanced }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_exceptional_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.quality_exceptional }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_quality_roll_bias = yes
	random_list = {
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_poor_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.poor }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_standard_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.standard }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_advanced_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.advanced }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_exceptional_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.exceptional }
		}
	}

	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_chaotic_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.coherence_chaotic }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mixed_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.coherence_mixed }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_coherent_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.coherence_coherent }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_specialist_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.coherence_specialist }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_coherence_roll_bias = yes
	random_list = {
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_chaotic_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode.chaotic }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mixed_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode.mixed }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_coherent_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode.coherent }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_specialist_weight = {

		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_safe_target_counts = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_tiny_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.combat_tiny }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_light_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.combat_light }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_full_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.combat_full }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_heavy_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.combat_heavy }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_bloated_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.combat_bloated }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_combat_count_roll_bias = yes
	random_list = {
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_tiny_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_minimum value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_tiny_minimum }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_maximum_exclusive value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_tiny_maximum_exclusive }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_light_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_minimum value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_light_minimum }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_maximum_exclusive value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_light_maximum_exclusive }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_full_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_minimum value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_full_minimum }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_maximum_exclusive value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_full_maximum_exclusive }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_heavy_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_minimum value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_heavy_minimum }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_maximum_exclusive value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_heavy_maximum_exclusive }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_bloated_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_minimum value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_bloated_minimum }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_maximum_exclusive value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_bloated_maximum_exclusive }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
		var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_combat_count
		min = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_minimum
		max = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_combat_roll_maximum_exclusive
		integer = yes
	}

	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_zero_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.support_zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_one_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.support_one }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_two_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.support_two }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_three_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.support_three }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_four_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.support_four }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_five_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_weight.support_five }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_support_count_roll_bias = yes
	random_list = {
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_zero_weight = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_support_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_support_one_weight = {

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_find_current_group = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.missing_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group_scan_break value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	for_each_loop = {
		array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_ids
		value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_group_id
		index = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_group_index
		break = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group_scan_break
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_group_id
					value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_counts^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_group_index
					value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_group_row_maximum
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_group_index }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_counts^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_group_index }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group_scan_break value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_quality_weight = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.poor compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.renewable compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.poor_renewable }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.constrained compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.poor_constrained }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.advanced compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.poor_advanced }
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.poor_project }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.standard compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.renewable compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.standard_renewable }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.constrained compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.standard_constrained }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.advanced compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.standard_advanced }
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.standard_project }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.advanced compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.renewable compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.advanced_renewable }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.constrained compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.advanced_constrained }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.advanced compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.advanced_advanced }
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.advanced_project }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.renewable compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.exceptional_renewable }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.constrained compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.exceptional_constrained }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.advanced compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.exceptional_advanced }
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_factor.exceptional_project }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_coherence_weight = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_anchor_compatibility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode.chaotic compare = equals }
			}
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_chaotic_coherence_weight = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode.mixed compare = equals }
			}
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_mixed_coherence_weight = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_coherence_mode.coherent compare = equals }
			}
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_coherent_coherence_weight = yes
		}
		else = {
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_specialist_coherence_weight = yes
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_request_candidate_weight = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.field_reinforcement compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.infantry compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.combat_support compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.support compare = equals }
				}
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.preferred }
		}
		else = { multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.discouraged } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.mobile_reserve compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.animal compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.motorized compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.mechanized compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.tracked compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.amphibious compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.airborne compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.helicopter compare = equals }
				}
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.preferred }
		}
		else = { multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.discouraged } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.territorial_defense compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.infantry compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.combat_support compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.support compare = equals }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.foot compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.animal compare = equals }
					}
				}
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.preferred }
		}
		else = { multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.discouraged } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.specialist compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_quality_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_weight.specialist compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_quality_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_weight.project compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_compatibility.specialist_foot compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_compatibility.recon compare = equals }
				}
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.preferred }
		}
		else = { multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.discouraged } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.numbers compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burden.minimal compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burden.light compare = equals }
				}
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.numbers_light_supply }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burden.heavy compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burden.extreme compare = equals }
				}
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.numbers_heavy_supply }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.firepower compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.combat_support compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.mobile_combat_support compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.armor compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.armor_combat_support compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_group.support compare = equals }
				}
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.preferred }
		}
		else = { multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.discouraged } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.mobility compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mobility.foot compare = equals } }
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.preferred }
		}
		else = { multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.discouraged } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.anything compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.advanced compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.anything_advanced }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.project compare = equals } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_factor.anything_project }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_calculate_current_candidate_weight = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_quality_weight }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_quality_weight = yes
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_coherence_weight = yes
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_apply_request_candidate_weight = yes
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_build_candidates = {
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_row_indices
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weights
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_total_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	for_each_loop = {
		array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_row_ids
		value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_row_id
		index = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_current_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_slot_kind value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_slot_kind.combat compare = equals }
			}
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_find_current_group = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_candidate_is_eligible = yes
			}
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_calculate_current_candidate_weight = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight
						value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.candidate_minimum_weight
						compare = greater_than
					}
				}
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_row_indices value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weights value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight }
				add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_total_weight value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weight }
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_record_current_candidate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_slot_kind value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_slot_kind.combat compare = equals }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_find_current_group = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.missing_index compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_ids^num }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_counts value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group_index }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_counts^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group_index }
		add_to_temp_variable = {
			var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_group_counts^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group_index
			value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_anchor_compatibility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_anchor_compatibility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_anchor_mobility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_count }
		add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_token_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_token_id }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_primary value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_same_support_type.none compare = not_equals }
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_type_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_primary }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_secondary value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_same_support_type.none compare = not_equals }
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_type_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_secondary }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_tertiary value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_same_support_type.none compare = not_equals }
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_type_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_tertiary }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}

	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_row_indices value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_token_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_token_id }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_static_tokens value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_static_token }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_provider_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_provider_id }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_slot_kinds value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_slot_kind }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_groups value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_columns value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_rows value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_row }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_equipment_profiles value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_equipment_profile }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_quality_weights value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_quality_weight }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_compatibility_classes value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_mobility_classes value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_supply_burdens value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_finite_risks value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_same_support_type_primary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_primary }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_same_support_type_secondary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_secondary }
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_same_support_type_tertiary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_tertiary }
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_select_weighted_candidate = {
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
		var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_roll
		min = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero
		max = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_total_weight
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_cumulative_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.missing_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_scan_break value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	for_each_loop = {
		array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_row_indices
		value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_candidate_row_index
		index = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_candidate_index
		break = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_scan_break
		add_to_temp_variable = {
			var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_cumulative_weight
			value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_weights^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_candidate_index
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_roll
					value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_cumulative_weight
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_row_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scanned_candidate_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_scan_break value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_row_index
				value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.missing_index
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_row_index }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_current_row = yes
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_record_current_candidate = yes
	}
}

# Inputs: target_combat_count, target_support_count, quality_tier, coherence_mode.
# Output: aligned infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_* temporary arrays.
infantry_spawn_unit_registry_build_manifest_from_targets = {
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_combat_count
		min = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_minimum
		max = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_maximum
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_support_count
		min = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.support_minimum
		max = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.support_maximum
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_component_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_combat_count }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_component_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_support_count }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_reset_selection = yes

	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_requested_slot_kind value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_slot_kind.combat }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_build_candidates = yes
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_combat_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_combat_count compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_total_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.candidate_minimum_weight compare = greater_than }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_select_weighted_candidate = yes
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_build_candidates = yes
	}

	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_requested_slot_kind value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_slot_kind.support }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_build_candidates = yes
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_selected_support_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_target_support_count compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_candidate_total_weight value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.candidate_minimum_weight compare = greater_than }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_select_weighted_candidate = yes
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_build_candidates = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_build_fully_random_manifest = {
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_initialize_tables = yes
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_independent_axes = yes
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_roll_safe_target_counts = yes
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_build_manifest_from_targets = yes
}

# Input: infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index.
# Output: current_* metadata and current_static_token for root-owned template wiring.
infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_manifest_row = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_row_indices^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_token_id value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_token_ids^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_static_token value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_static_tokens^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_provider_id value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_provider_ids^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_slot_kind value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_slot_kinds^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_groups^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_columns^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_rows^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_equipment_profile value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_equipment_profiles^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_quality_weight value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_quality_weights^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_compatibility_classes^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_mobility_classes^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_supply_burdens^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_finite_risks^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_primary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_same_support_type_primary^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_secondary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_same_support_type_secondary^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_tertiary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_same_support_type_tertiary^infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index }
}
