infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag_ids
					value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_array = {
			array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag_ids
			value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_reset_public_result = {
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_reset_selection = yes
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag_ids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_group_ids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_equipment_profile_ids
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_combat_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_support_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_distinct_group_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_distinct_equipment_profile_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_compatibility_mismatch_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_mobility_mismatch_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_incoherence_score value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_heavy_supply_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_extreme_supply_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_max_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_finite_risk_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_project_equipment_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_technology_locked_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_unresolved_variant_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_compatibility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialized_lot_uid value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.missing_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialized_lot_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.missing_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialized_template_uid value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.missing_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialized_unit_uid value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.missing_index }
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialization_succeeded
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_score_current_manifest_row = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_slot_kind value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_slot_kind.combat compare = equals }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_combat_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_compatibility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_compatibility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_mobility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility }
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_support_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_group_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group } }
		}
		add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_group_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_equipment_profile_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_equipment_profile } }
		}
		add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_equipment_profile_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_equipment_profile }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_compatibility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = not_equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_compatibility compare = not_equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_compatibility.universal_service compare = not_equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_compatibility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_compatibility.universal_service compare = not_equals }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_compatibility_mismatch_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_mobility value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = not_equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_anchor_mobility compare = not_equals }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_mobility_mismatch_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burden.heavy compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_heavy_supply_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burden.extreme compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_extreme_supply_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_max_supply_burden compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_max_supply_burden value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.renewable compare = greater_than } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_finite_risk_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_finite_equipment_risk.project compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_project_equipment_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_equipment_is_technology_locked = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_technology_locked_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_equipment_variant_is_unresolved = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_unresolved_variant_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_score_manifest = {
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_group_ids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_equipment_profile_ids
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_token_ids^num compare = less_than }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_manifest_row = yes
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_score_current_manifest_row = yes
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_distinct_group_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_group_ids^num }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_distinct_equipment_profile_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_seen_equipment_profile_ids^num }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_incoherence_score value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_compatibility_mismatch_count }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_incoherence_score value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_mobility_mismatch_count }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_incoherence_score value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.coherent }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_incoherence_score value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.incoherent_mismatch_minimum compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.strained }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.incoherent }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_combat_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_bloated_minimum compare = less_than } } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.bloated }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_combat_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.combat_tiny_maximum_exclusive compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.tiny }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_technology_locked_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.technology_locked }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_unresolved_variant_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.equipment_variant_unresolved }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_heavy_supply_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_extreme_supply_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.heavy_supply }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_project_equipment_component_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.project_equipment }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_mobility_mismatch_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.mobility_mismatch }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.draw_complete }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
}

# Inputs: request_mode and request_context. Both use their public constant enums.
# Outputs: manifest arrays, result counters, and outcome_tag_ids.
infantry_spawn_unit_registry_draw_requested_lot = {
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_reset_public_result = yes
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_has_country_safe_origin = no }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.invalid_origin }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_inputs_are_valid = no }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.invalid_request }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
	else = {
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_build_fully_random_manifest = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				infantry_spawn_unit_registry_tables_are_aligned = yes
				infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_is_complete = yes
			}
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_score_manifest = yes
		}
		else = {
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_reset_selection = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.manifest_incomplete }
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
		}
	}
}

# One opening Evolution III draw for an already-selected safe origin state.
infantry_spawn_unit_registry_draw_evolution_iii_opening_lot = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.unrestricted }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_context value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_context.evolution_iii_opening }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_command_owner value = constant:infantry_spawn_command_owner.state }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_draw_requested_lot = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Safe manifest materialization
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_prepare_current_lot_readiness = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.exceptional compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_quality value = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.exceptional }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_training value = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.field_ready }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_equipment_state value = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.substantially_equipped }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_experience value = constant:infantry_spawn_readiness.serviceable_experience }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_initial_factor.exceptional_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_manpower value = constant:infantry_spawn_initial_factor.exceptional_manpower }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.advanced compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_quality value = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.serviceable }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_training value = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.serviceable }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_equipment_state value = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.substantially_equipped }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_experience value = constant:infantry_spawn_readiness.serviceable_experience }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_initial_factor.serviceable_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_manpower value = constant:infantry_spawn_initial_factor.serviceable_manpower }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_quality_tier.standard compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_quality value = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.mixed }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_training value = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.partially_trained }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_equipment_state value = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.mixed_issue }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_experience value = constant:infantry_spawn_readiness.partial_experience }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_initial_factor.mixed_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_manpower value = constant:infantry_spawn_initial_factor.mixed_manpower }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_quality value = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.poor }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_training value = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.minimally_drilled }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_equipment_state value = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.understrength }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_experience value = constant:infantry_spawn_readiness.improvised_experience }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_initial_factor.poor_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_start_manpower value = constant:infantry_spawn_initial_factor.poor_manpower }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag_ids value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.incoherent } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_coherence value = constant:infantry_spawn_coherence.absurd }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag_ids value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.strained } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_coherence value = constant:infantry_spawn_coherence.strained }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_coherence value = constant:infantry_spawn_coherence.coherent } }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_max_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burden.extreme compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_supply value = constant:infantry_spawn_supply_burden.locally_impossible }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_max_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burden.heavy compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_supply value = constant:infantry_spawn_supply_burden.heavy }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_max_supply_burden value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_supply_burden.moderate compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_supply value = constant:infantry_spawn_supply_burden.ordinary }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_supply value = constant:infantry_spawn_supply_burden.light } }

	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_mobility_mismatch value = constant:infantry_spawn_mobility_mismatch.none }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_mobility_mismatch_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.incoherent_mismatch_minimum compare = less_than } } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_mobility_mismatch value = constant:infantry_spawn_mobility_mismatch.severe }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_mobility_mismatch_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_mobility_mismatch value = constant:infantry_spawn_mobility_mismatch.moderate }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_create_engine_template_from_manifest = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_manifest_row = yes
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			division_template = {
				name = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]"
				is_locked = yes
				force_allow_recruiting = no
				regiments = {
					[UNIT_TOKEN] = { x = [COLUMN] y = [ROW] }
				}
			}
		}
		TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_template_uid|.0]"
		UNIT_TOKEN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_static_token.GetTokenKey]"
		COLUMN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column|.0]"
		ROW = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_row|.0]"
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_token_ids^num compare = less_than }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_manifest_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_slot_kind value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_slot_kind.combat compare = equals } }
			meta_effect = {
				text = {
					add_units_to_division_template = {
						template_name = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]"
						regiments = { [UNIT_TOKEN] = [COLUMN] }
					}
				}
				TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_template_uid|.0]"
				UNIT_TOKEN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_static_token.GetTokenKey]"
				COLUMN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column|.0]"
			}
		}
		else = {
			meta_effect = {
				text = {
					add_units_to_division_template = {
						template_name = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]"
						support = { [UNIT_TOKEN] = [COLUMN] }
					}
				}
				TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_template_uid|.0]"
				UNIT_TOKEN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_static_token.GetTokenKey]"
				COLUMN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column|.0]"
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_append_manifest_component_ledger = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_token_ids^num compare = less_than }
		}
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_manifest_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_component_slot_kind value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_slot_kind }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_component_unit_profile value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_token_id }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_component_column value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_component_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_row }
		infantry_spawn_append_current_template_component = yes
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_generation_context = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_generation_profile value = infantry_spawn_generation_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_eligible_state_count value = infantry_spawn_generation_eligible_state_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_selected_state_count value = infantry_spawn_generation_selected_state_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_generation_lot_count value = infantry_spawn_generation_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_generation_unit_count value = infantry_spawn_generation_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_generation_status value = infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mark_current_materialization_technology_locked = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag_ids value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.technology_locked }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } } }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid } } }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } } }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid } } }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_technology_locked_formation
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_pretechnology_formation
	}
}

# The transactional spawner keeps the exact newly created division in this
# short-lived event target. Persist the scored manifest on that live division
# so achievement, transfer, and isolation checks do not have to infer identity
# from template names or country-level totals.
infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mark_current_live_division_manifest = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = infantry_spawn_current_live_division }
		event_target:infantry_spawn_current_live_division = {
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_distinct_group_count = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_distinct_group_count }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_distinct_equipment_profile_count = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_distinct_equipment_profile_count }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_compatibility_mismatch_count = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_compatibility_mismatch_count }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_incoherence_score = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_incoherence_score }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_heavy_supply_component_count = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_heavy_supply_component_count }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_extreme_supply_component_count = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_extreme_supply_component_count }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_finite_risk_component_count = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_finite_risk_component_count }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_project_equipment_component_count = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_project_equipment_component_count }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_technology_locked_component_count = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_technology_locked_component_count }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unresolved_variant_component_count = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_unresolved_variant_component_count }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_materialization_failure_tag = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag_ids value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.materialized } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.materialization_failed }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
	}
}

# Inputs: a successful draw, current_generation_uid, both safe-origin event
# targets, and request_command_owner. Outputs: lot/template/unit UIDs, lot index,
# the materialized outcome tag, and a country success flag.
infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialize_drawn_lot = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialization_inputs_are_valid = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index compare = greater_than }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.open compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.audited compare = equals }
				}
			}
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_generation_context = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_generation_status value = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.open }
			infantry_spawn_allocate_lot_uid = yes
			infantry_spawn_allocate_template_uid = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_family_id value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_lot_profile value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_lot_profile.evolution_iii_random }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_command_owner value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_command_owner }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_lot_status value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.auditing }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_template_status value = constant:infantry_spawn_template_status.locked }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_recruitment_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_recruitment_mode.locked_pending }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_combat_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_combat_count }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_support_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_support_count }
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_prepare_current_lot_readiness = yes
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_create_engine_template_from_manifest = yes
			infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_template_exists value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero compare = greater_than } }
				infantry_spawn_unit_registry_append_manifest_component_ledger = yes
				infantry_spawn_count_current_template_components = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						infantry_spawn_template_component_ledger_is_aligned = yes
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_combat_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_combat_count compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_support_count value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_result_support_count compare = equals }
					}
					infantry_spawn_append_current_lot_row = yes
					infantry_spawn_append_current_template_row = yes
					infantry_spawn_append_selected_state_row = yes
					add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_selected_state_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure } }
						infantry_spawn_unit_registry_reset_extended_need_accumulators = yes
						infantry_spawn_spawn_current_template_unit = yes
						set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lookup_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid }
						infantry_spawn_find_unit_row = yes
						if = {
							limit = {
								NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
								check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index compare = greater_than }
							}
							infantry_spawn_unit_registry_scale_extended_need_accumulators = yes
							infantry_spawn_unit_registry_append_extended_obligations = yes
							if = {
								limit = {
									NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
									infantry_spawn_obligation_ledger_is_aligned = yes
								}
								infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mark_current_materialization_technology_locked = yes
								infantry_spawn_unit_registry_mark_current_live_division_manifest = yes
								infantry_spawn_update_current_generation_totals = yes
								set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid }
								infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
								if = {
									limit = {
										NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
										check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index compare = greater_than }
									}
									set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialized_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid }
									set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialized_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
									set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialized_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid }
									set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialized_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid }
									set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_generation_audit_active
									set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialization_succeeded
									set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.materialized }
									infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
								}
								else = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_materialization_failure_tag = yes }
							}
							else = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_materialization_failure_tag = yes }
						}
						else = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_materialization_failure_tag = yes }
					}
					else = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_materialization_failure_tag = yes }
				}
				else = {
					infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
					infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_materialization_failure_tag = yes
				}
			}
			else = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_materialization_failure_tag = yes }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_outcome_tag value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.invalid_generation_context }
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_current_outcome_tag = yes
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_materialization_failure_tag = yes
		}
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_add_materialization_failure_tag = yes }
}

# Exact vanilla obligation extensions

# These providers complete the dynamic dispatch used by the Event 19 ordinary
# obligation scanner. The providers already present in generation effects are
# intentionally not redefined here.

# Exact obligation provider 1: amphibious_armor
infantry_spawn_accumulate_component_needs_1 = {
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_need_manpower value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_exact_need.amphibious_armor__manpower }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_need_amphibious_tank_chassis value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_exact_need.amphibious_armor__amphibious_tank_chassis }
}

# ============================================================================
# EVENT 002 PROVIDER - EVENT 019 BASE ZOMBIE FAMILY
# ============================================================================
# This file reuses only the base battalion token. It does not call Zombie
# Outbreak setup, recruitment profiles, mutation, league, evolution, or world-
# end effects.
# ============================================================================

# COUNTRY scope. The three initial providers share only the authority shell.
# The one-time receipt prevents a later final-cleanup pass from retiring an
# unrelated leader created after the defeat callback already removed the
# provider-owned army-host authority.
chaos_unit_family_event19_cleanup_initial_provider_authority = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_authority_removed } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_leadership_installed }
			retire_country_leader = yes
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_leadership_installed
		}
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_authority_removed
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_501_event19_remove_public_additions = {
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_fragmented_command
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_bound_to_commander
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_coordinated_bands
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_instinct_hierarchy
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_barracks_scavenged
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_bands_numbered
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_training_operation_unlocked
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_hunger_disciplined
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_identity_complete
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_training_authorized
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_trainable_family_ids value = constant:event19_zombie_family.family_id } }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_trainable_family_ids value = constant:event19_zombie_family.family_id }
	}
	# Lock every recorded family-501 template, including a trainable template
	# whose last live division was destroyed before defeat.
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_index value = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_is_zombie = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_is_zombie < constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_lot_index value = constant:event19_zombie_family.family_id compare = equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_is_zombie = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_is_zombie > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			meta_effect = {
				text = {
					set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" force_allow_recruiting = no }
					set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" is_locked = yes }
				}
				TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_uid|.0]"
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = infantry_spawn_derivative_owned_marker_states
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid value = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid compare = equals }
			}
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_muster_district
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_population_pressure
		}
	}
	chaos_unit_family_event19_cleanup_initial_provider_authority = yes
}

# Event 19 registry-management contract. Base zombies are the only zombie
# family exposed as trainable; this path authorizes their exact generated
# template and never unlocks a mutated or weaponized zombie battalion.
chaos_unit_family_provider_501_event19_evaluate_management = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_train = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_spawn = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_sustain = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_uses_training = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_trainable_family_ids value = constant:event19_zombie_family.family_id } }
			infantry_spawn_country_has_eligible_state = yes
			check_variable = { var = army_experience value = constant:event19_zombie_family.training_army_experience_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_equipment value = constant:event19_zombie_family.training_infantry_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = manpower value = constant:event19_zombie_family.training_manpower_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_train = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_live_division_count > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_equipment value = constant:event19_zombie_family.sustainment_infantry_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = manpower value = constant:event19_zombie_family.sustainment_manpower_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_sustain = 1 }
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_501_event19_pay_management_action = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.train_or_spawn }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_train > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_zombie_family.training_army_experience_cost }
		army_experience = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_zombie_family.training_infantry_equipment_cost }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_zombie_family.training_manpower_cost }
		add_manpower = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.sustainment }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_sustain > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_zombie_family.sustainment_infantry_equipment_cost }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_zombie_family.sustainment_manpower_cost }
		add_manpower = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 1 }
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_501_event19_refund_management_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.train_or_spawn }
		}
		add_army_experience = constant:event19_zombie_family.training_army_experience_cost
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:event19_zombie_family.training_infantry_equipment_cost }
		add_manpower = constant:event19_zombie_family.training_manpower_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.sustainment }
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:event19_zombie_family.sustainment_infantry_equipment_cost }
		add_manpower = constant:event19_zombie_family.sustainment_manpower_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_501_event19_setup_derivative = {
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_parent_event_identity_is_absent = yes }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
		infantry_spawn_setup_zombie_derivative_identity = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_release_report_dispatched
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_501_event19_cleanup_derivative = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative
			infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = constant:event19_zombie_family.family_id }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id = constant:event19_zombie_family.provider_id }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cleanup_phase = constant:chaos_unit_family_derivative_cleanup_phase.defeat }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cleanup_phase = constant:chaos_unit_family_derivative_cleanup_phase.final }
			}
		}
		chaos_unit_family_provider_501_event19_remove_public_additions = yes
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_cleanup_proved
	}
}

	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_unstable_manifestation
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_bound_procession
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_chorus_of_anchors
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_haunting_without_masters

	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_anchor_network_established
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline_cooldown
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = infantry_spawn_derivative_owned_marker_states
			limit = {

			}
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_recognized_anchor
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_population_pressure
		}
	}
	chaos_unit_family_event19_cleanup_initial_provider_authority = yes
}

		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.train_or_spawn }
		}
		add_political_power = constant:event19_ghost_family.manifest_political_power_cost
		add_command_power = constant:event19_ghost_family.manifest_command_power_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.sustainment }
		}
		add_political_power = constant:event19_ghost_family.sustainment_political_power_cost
		add_command_power = constant:event19_ghost_family.sustainment_command_power_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_502_event19_setup_derivative = {
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_parent_event_identity_is_absent = yes }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
		infantry_spawn_setup_ghost_derivative_identity = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_release_report_dispatched
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_502_event19_cleanup_derivative = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = constant:event19_ghost_family.family_id }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id = constant:event19_ghost_family.provider_id }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cleanup_phase = constant:chaos_unit_family_derivative_cleanup_phase.defeat }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cleanup_phase = constant:chaos_unit_family_derivative_cleanup_phase.final }
			}
		}
		chaos_unit_family_provider_502_event19_remove_public_additions = yes
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_cleanup_proved
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_503_event19_remove_public_additions = {
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_broken_pattern
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_master_bound_constructs
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_distributed_binding
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_living_pattern
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_coal_recovered
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_binding_operation_unlocked
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_foundries_open
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_pattern_shared
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_identity_complete
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = infantry_spawn_derivative_owned_marker_states
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid value = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid compare = equals }
			}
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_bound_district
		}
	}
	chaos_unit_family_event19_cleanup_initial_provider_authority = yes
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_503_event19_evaluate_eligibility = {
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_candidate_eligible = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_candidate_native = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = KMB has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative } }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_candidate_eligible = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_candidate_native = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_has_evolution_iv = yes
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_scenario_force_evolution_iv
				any_controlled_state = { industrial_complex > constant:event19_golem_family.minimum_industrial_state_factories }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_candidate_eligible = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_candidate_native = 1 }
	}
}

		text = {
			division_template = {
				name = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]"
				is_locked = yes
				force_allow_recruiting = no
				priority = 0
				regiments = {
					coal_golem = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					coal_golem = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_template_uid|.0]"
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_component_count = constant:event19_golem_family.template_battalions }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_provider_component_profile = constant:event19_golem_family.family_id }
	infantry_spawn_append_provider_combat_column_manifest = yes
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_503_event19_evaluate_management = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_train = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_spawn = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_sustain = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_uses_training = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_political_power < constant:event19_golem_family.binding_political_power_cost }
			NOT = { command_power < constant:event19_golem_family.binding_command_power_cost }
			NOT = { num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects < constant:event19_golem_family.binding_civilian_factory_gate }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@coal_golem_equipment_1 value = constant:event19_golem_family.binding_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			infantry_spawn_country_has_eligible_state = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_spawn = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_live_division_count > 0 }
			NOT = { has_political_power < constant:event19_golem_family.sustainment_political_power_cost }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@coal_golem_equipment_1 value = constant:event19_golem_family.sustainment_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_sustain = 1 }
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_503_event19_pay_management_action = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.train_or_spawn }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_spawn > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_golem_family.binding_political_power_cost }
		add_political_power = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_golem_family.binding_command_power_cost }
		add_command_power = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_golem_family.binding_equipment_cost }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = coal_golem_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.sustainment }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_sustain > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_golem_family.sustainment_political_power_cost }
		add_political_power = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost = constant:event19_golem_family.sustainment_equipment_cost }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = coal_golem_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_family_provider_negative_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 1 }
	}
}

chaos_unit_family_provider_503_event19_refund_management_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.train_or_spawn }
		}
		add_political_power = constant:event19_golem_family.binding_political_power_cost
		add_command_power = constant:event19_golem_family.binding_command_power_cost
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = coal_golem_equipment_1 amount = constant:event19_golem_family.binding_equipment_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.sustainment }
		}
		add_political_power = constant:event19_golem_family.sustainment_political_power_cost
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = coal_golem_equipment_1 amount = constant:event19_golem_family.sustainment_equipment_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	}
}
